    "description": "*created by [@FlohGro](https://twitter.com/FlohGro) / more on my [Blog](http://www.flohgro.com/blog)*\n\n## Launch Action\n\nThis action can be used as a launcher of Actions from an ActionGroup in Drafts.\n\nYou can configure the action to always show the actions from one Action Group in a prompt - the selected action will be launched.\n\nIf you don’t specify an Action Group the action will first present a prompt where you have to select the Action Group and afterwards prompt for the Action you want to run.\n\n## [Configuration]\n\n\nTo configure the action to use a specific Action Group you have to edit the „Define Template Step“ in the action. In the Text field „Template“ replace `undefined` with the name of the Action Group which shall be used by the action.\n\nTo let the action query for all Action Groups set it back to „undefined“.\n\nIf you want to use this action for several Action Groups, just duplicate it and configure the names of the Action Groups.\n\nI recommend to add a suffix to the action name to identify the Action Group you configured.\n\n## [Usage]\n\n\nUse this Action to quickly launch an action from a specific Action Group or all your Action Groups.\n\nYou can assign it to a keyboard shortcut or add it to an action bar. \n\n---\n\n*If you find this useful and want to support me you can* \n\n<a href=\"https://www.buymeacoffee.com/flohgro\" target=\"_blank\"><img src=\"https://cdn.buymeacoffee.com/buttons/v2/default-blue.png\" alt=\"Buy Me A Coffee\" style=\"height: 51px !important;width: 209px !important;\" ></a>",

It will run the action afterwards.\n\nThis is useful for the action bar or an action group where you want to combine different actions without making several aliases of them.\n\n### [Configuration]\n\nThe action uses the newly introduced (January 2025) \"Configure Value\" steps.     "classification": "System: Draft Management",
    "description": "**Tagger Overview:**  \n  \nThis Drafts Action allows efficient tag management, helping you organize your drafts with precision. It reads a pre-created \"master tag list\" (stored as a separate draft where each tag is listed on a new line) and presents these tags in a selectable list. You can use *Multiple Keyword Filtering* by separating your search terms with commas. For instance, typing `fru` will filter the list to show tags such as `[sne*fru*]`, `[jack*fru*its]`, and `[*fru*gal]`.  \n  \n### Note:  \nYou need to input the UUID of your \"master tag list\" draft in the second line of the second script:  \n`Draft.find(\"UUID\")`  \n  \n---  \n  \n### Process Workflow  \n1. **Decide on Existing Tags:**  \n   - Choose to either keep or remove the tags currently assigned to the draft.  \n   - Enter comma-separated filter keywords to narrow down the master list.  \n   - Leave the input field blank to display the full tag list.  \n  \n2. **Filter Tags:**  \n   - Apply the filter keywords to the master tag list.   \n   - Retain the currently assigned tags in the final list only if you chose to keep them in Step 1.  \n  \n3. **Tag Selection:**  \n   - View the filtered list of tags, including any retained ones.  \n   - Select from the filtered options and, optionally, manually add additional tags.  \n  \n4. **Update the Draft:**  \n   - Once your tags are finalized, update the draft with the selected tags along with any manually added ones. This action won't work if you don't have that installed (with apologies to Matt for my own less-than-elegant hackery around his brilliant work...;)\n\nThe action queries your drafts for the search term you specify, or linked/unlinked references to the current draft. Also... \n- any text selected when the action is invoked will be used as the search term (no prompt)\n- if no text is selected, and the cursor is in a line with a wiki-linked phrase, that wiki-linked phrase is offered as an option for the search— if there's more than one, each wiki-link will be offered as an option \n\nResponses to your search query are loaded into an interface (the Matt Gemmell Checklist Prompt), displaying **the entirety of each line/paragraph (aka \"block\") that the search term is found in** for context. \n\nSelecting an option from the list should load the appropriate draft and select/highlight the correct instance of that search term in the loaded draft. \n\nNOTES: \n- The action currently has one setting for easy adjustment: if you'd prefer that the list shows the full text of a draft if the term you're searching for is found in the title of that draft, set the variable in the first action step to be true. Otherwise, leave it false.\n- The search that this action performs is an “exact phrase” search, meaning that if you search for a phrase consisting of more than one word, you’ll only see results containing exactly that phrase, rather than results that contain each of the words in that phrase. To borrow an example from [Drafts’ guide on search and filtering](https://docs.getdrafts.com/docs/drafts/filtering): \"red parachute\" will find a draft with the sentence “She used a red parachute when skydiving”, but not the draft “She used a red and blue parachute…” None of the other advanced query options detailed in that guide are enabled here.",

    "classification": "System: Clipboard",
    "description": "*created by [@FlohGro](https://twitter.com/FlohGro) / more on my [Blog](http://www.flohgro.com/blog)*\n\n## Craft Document From Template\n\nThis action creates documents in Craft from templates stored in Drafts.\n\nYou can setup as many templates as you want. They are identified from the action by one or more configurable tags you apply to the template drafts. The action also supports you to easily create new templates with your configured tags.\n\nThe action will query for all drafts with the configured tag(s) (its optionally possible to configure tags which shall be omitted). A prompt will be displayed where you can select the template you want to use.\n\nAfter selecting a template the action will create and open the new document in Craft.\n\n## [Configuration]\n\n\nBefore you can use the action you have to configure it to your needs as follows.\n\n\n- **template tags:** you have to configure the tags which your templates should include (the action will only display these drafts in the menu)\n\t- therefore you have to edit the script step of the action\n\t- in line 4 `const templateTags = [\"3 template\", \"3 sw craft\"];` you will see the constant „templateTags“\n\t\t- this constant defines the tags which identify your craft template drafts. Please add them as comma-separated-strings into the array.\n\t\t- feel free to delete the standard configuration of `\"3 template\", \"3 sw craft\"`\n\t\t- make sure to at at least one tag into the array\n- *[optional]* **template omit tags:** you can configure tags which should not be included in the drafts found by this action\n\t- e.g. you have configured your templateTags to \"3 template\", \"3 sw craft“, but you have other drafts with the tag „todo“ which should be ignored by this action\n\t- add these tags into the empty array of the constant „templateOmitTags“ in line 7 `const templateOmitTags = [];` and the corresponding drafts containing this tag will be ignored by this action\n\nWhen you first run any of my Craft actions requiring the space id it will ask you to store the space id of your Craft space. This is a one time action and you don’t need to do it for any other of my Craft actions you install.\n\nTo retrieve your spaceId just copy the deeplink of any document in that space (refer to the [Craft Support Page](https://support.craft.do/hc/en-us/articles/360020043878-How-to-link-into-a-specific-place-in-Craft-with-a-Deeplink) when you don't know how to do that). Paste the copied id into a draft and you will se a link similar to this: \"craftdocs://open?blockId=[the block id]&spaceId=[the spaceId]\" - find the character combination \"[the spaceId]\" after the \"spaceId=\" and copy it and paste it into the prompt of this action.\n\nIf you don’t wan’t to use the (any of my) action for different Craft spaces there is no further configuration needed.\n\nTo use these actions with different spaces you need to duplicate the action for each space you want to use it. I recommend to e.g. add a suffix to the action name to describe the space for which you configure it.  \nThe action uses Drafts possibility to store [credentials](https://docs.getdrafts.com/docs/settings/credentials) to distinguish different spaces. When you duplicate the action for another space you have to change the name of the credential. Therefore you need edit this line `const spaceIdCredentialName = \"CraftDocumentSpace\"` in the script step of the action and change the `CraftDocumentSpace` to something different (e.g. describe the space in a suffix like „CraftDocumentSpacePersonal\" or „CraftDocumentSpaceWork“. If you use several of my Craft actions you should use the same credential name in all of them.\n\n## [Usage]\n\n\n### Setting up templates\n\n\nWhen you finished the [Configuration] you can start setting up your templates. If you already have drafts with the configured tags they will directly show up in the prompt of the action.\n\nIf you don't have any draft with the configured tag(s), the prompt will just display a button \"[Create New Template]\" when you select that button, Drafts will create a new Draft with the configured tags and a default content.\n\nReplace the title with the name of your template (which will be the title of the created Craft Document) and just insert the content you want to have in that template in markdown format\n\nSetup as many templates as you want\n\n### Creating Documents from Templates\n\n\nWhen you configured the action and set up one or more templates the action can be used very easily.\n\nWhen you run it the prompt will display the titles of all drafts with the configured tags, just select the template you want to use and the action will create and directly open the new document in Craft.\n\n\n---\n\n*If you find this useful and want to support me you can* \n\n<a href=\"https://www.buymeacoffee.com/flohgro\" target=\"_blank\"><img src=\"https://cdn.buymeacoffee.com/buttons/v2/default-blue.png\" alt=\"Buy Me A Coffee\" style=\"height: 51px !important;width: 209px !important;\" ></a>",

It will be stored in Drafts credentials - if you run into issues with the API you can „forget“ the credential in Drafts Settings\n\n\nYou need to set the target language (the language you want your draft to be translated to). By default this is `EN` but you can change that to one of the options below. Please only insert the two letter language code into the „Define Template Tag“ step for `target_language`\n\nFollowing options are available as target language:\n\n- `BG` - Bulgarian\n- `CS` - Czech\n- `DA` - Danish\n- `DE` - German\n- `EL` - Greek\n- `EN` - English (unspecified variant for backward compatibility; please select `EN-GB` or `EN-US`instead)\n- `EN-GB` - English (British)\n- `EN-US` - English (American)\n- `ES` - Spanish\n- `ET` - Estonian\n- `FI` - Finnish\n- `FR` - French\n- `HU` - Hungarian\n- `ID` - Indonesian\n- `IT` - Italian\n- `JA` - Japanese\n- `KO` - Korean\n- `LT` - Lithuanian\n- `LV` - Latvian\n- `NB` - Norwegian (Bokmål)\n- `NL` - Dutch\n- `PL` - Polish\n- `PT` - Portuguese (unspecified variant for backward compatibility; please select `PT-BR` or `PT-PT` instead)\n- `PT-BR` - Portuguese (Brazilian)\n- `PT-PT` - Portuguese (all Portuguese varieties excluding Brazilian Portuguese)\n- `RO` - Romanian\n- `RU` - Russian\n- `SK` - Slovak\n- `SL` - Slovenian\n- `SV` - Swedish\n- `TR` - Turkish\n- `UK` - Ukrainian\n- `ZH` - Chinese (simplified)\n\nYou can always check an up-to-date list in the [DeepL API reference](https://www.deepl.com/docs-api/translate-text/translate-text/)\n\nBy default the Action will append the translation to the current draft. If you want the Action to replace the current content of the draft with the translated text you can change the Template tag `result_insert_mode` to `replace`. Please note: only `append` and `replace` are valid values for this template tag. If you use something else, it will default to appending the text\n\n---\n\n*If you find this useful and want to support me you can [donate](https://flohgro.com/donate) or buy me a coffe* \n\n<a href=\"https://www.buymeacoffee.com/flohgro\" target=\"_blank\"><img src=\"https://cdn.buymeacoffee.com/buttons/v2/default-blue.png\" alt=\"Buy Me A Coffee\" style=\"height: 51px !important;width: 209px !important;\" ></a>\n\n",

    "classification": "System: Draft Management",
    "description": "Creates a new draft for each line in the current draft, with optional tagging support.\nFormat:\n\n````\nTitle (ignored)\n#globalTag1, #globalTag2 (optional — applied to all drafts)\nItem one #tag1, #tag2\nItem two #tag3\nItem three\n````\n\nRules:\n- The first line is treated as a title and ignored.\n- If the second line consists entirely of #-prefixed tags (comma or space-separated), those tags are applied to every draft created, and the line itself is not turned into a draft.\n- Each subsequent non-empty line becomes a new draft. Any #-prefixed strings after the content are added as tags for that draft, separated by commas or spaces before each #.\n- Empty lines are ignored.\n\nExample input:\n\n````\n# Summer Reading List\n#reading, #2026\nThe Left Hand of Darkness, Ursula K. Le Guin #sci-fi, #novel\nCitizen: An American Lyric, Claudia Rankine #poetry, #essential\nHow to Do Nothing, Jenny Odell #non-fiction, #attention\nSeveral Short Sentences About Writing, Verlyn Klinkenborg #craft, #writing\nThe Hatred of Poetry, Ben Lerner #poetry, #essay\nParable of the Sower, Octavia Butler #sci-fi, #novel\nIn the Dream House, Carmen Maria Machado #memoir, #non-fiction\n````\n\nResult: 7 drafts, all tagged reading and 2025, each individually tagged by genre/category.     "classification": "System: Draft Management",
    "description": "Creates a new draft for each line in the current draft, with optional tagging support.\nFormat:\n\n````\nTitle (ignored)\n#globalTag1, #globalTag2 (optional — applied to all drafts)\nItem one #tag1, #tag2\nItem two #tag3\nItem three\n````\n\nRules:\n- The first line is treated as a title and ignored.\n- If the second line consists entirely of #-prefixed tags (comma or space-separated), those tags are applied to every draft created, and the line itself is not turned into a draft.\n- Each subsequent non-empty line becomes a new draft.
